FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_momentum_walk_forward.py
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from backend.app.analysis.momentum_discovery import MomentumDiscoveryEngine
from backend.app.analysis.momentum_walk_forward import (
    MomentumWalkForwardEngine,
    MomentumWalkForwardRequest,
    sinyal_gecmisi_tablosu,
    walk_forward_tablosu,
)


class SahteAnalizMotoru:
    def veriden_analiz_et(self, sembol: str, fiyat_verisi: pd.DataFrame) -> object:
        return SimpleNamespace(
            sinyal=SimpleNamespace(skor=70, karar="Pozitif Teknik Görünüm"),
            risk_plani=SimpleNamespace(risk_odul_orani=2.0, filtreyi_gecti=True),
        )


def tarihsel_veri() -> pd.DataFrame:
    bar = 150
    x = np.arange(bar, dtype=float)
    kapanis = 40.0 + x * 0.04 + np.sin(x / 4.0) * 0.5
    # Kesim öncesinde dirence yakın, sıkışan ve hacim biriktiren yapı.
    direnc = float(kapanis[90:110].max())
    kapanis[110:120] = np.linspace(direnc * 0.96, direnc * 0.99, 10)
    # Gelecek hareket yalnızca performans ölçümünde kullanılacak.
    kapanis[120:] = np.linspace(kapanis[119] * 1.01, kapanis[119] * 1.18, 30)
    hacim = np.full(bar, 1_000_000.0)
    hacim[110:120] = np.linspace(1_050_000.0, 1_500_000.0, 10)
    hacim[120:] = 2_000_000.0
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "Open": kapanis - 0.10,
            "High": kapanis + 0.15,
            "Low": kapanis - 0.15,
            "Close": kapanis,
            "Volume": hacim,
        },
        index=pd.bdate_range("2025-01-01", periods=bar),
    )


def test_walk_forward_gelecek_veriyi_gizler_ve_performansi_olcer() -> None:
    veri = tarihsel_veri()
    kesim = str(veri.index[119].date())
    motor = MomentumWalkForwardEngine(
        MomentumDiscoveryEngine(analiz_motoru=SahteAnalizMotoru())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    sonuclar, hatalar = motor.calistir(
        {"AAA.IS": veri},
        kesim_tarihi=kesim,
        ufuklar=(5, 10, 20),
    )

    assert hatalar == ()
    assert len(sonuclar) == 1
    assert sonuclar[0].aday.analiz_tarihi == kesim
    assert sonuclar[0].getiri(5) is not None
    assert sonuclar[0].getiri(20) is not None
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_olumlu_hareket is not None
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_olumlu_hareket > 0.10
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_fiyat is not None
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_fiyat > float(veri.iloc[119]["Close"])
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_fiyat_tarihi
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_ters_hareket is not None
    assert sonuclar[0].maksimum_ters_hareket >= 0


def test_walk_forward_yetersiz_kesim_gecmisini_hata_yapar() -> None:
    veri = tarihsel_veri()
    kesim = str(veri.index[40].date())
    motor = MomentumWalkForwardEngine(
        MomentumDiscoveryEngine(analiz_motoru=SahteAnalizMotoru())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    sonuclar, hatalar = motor.calistir({"AAA.IS": veri}, kesim_tarihi=kesim)

    assert sonuclar == ()
    assert len(hatalar) == 1
    assert "en az 80" in hatalar[0].hata


def test_walk_forward_istegi_gecersiz_tarihi_reddeder() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="YYYY-AA-GG"):
        MomentumWalkForwardRequest(kesim_tarihi="27/07/2026")


def test_walk_forward_tablosu_ileri_ufuklari_gosterir() -> None:
    veri = tarihsel_veri()
    kesim = str(veri.index[119].date())
    motor = MomentumWalkForwardEngine(
        MomentumDiscoveryEngine(analiz_motoru=SahteAnalizMotoru())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    sonuclar, _ = motor.calistir({"AAA.IS": veri}, kesim_tarihi=kesim)
    sonuc = SimpleNamespace(sonuclar=sonuclar)

    tablo = walk_forward_tablosu(sonuc)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    assert tablo[0]["Hisse"] == "AAA"
    assert "5G kapanış (%)" in tablo[0]
    assert "Dönem içi en yüksek" in tablo[0]
    assert "Gerçek azami yükseliş (%)" in tablo[0]
    assert "Yakın direnç" in tablo[0]
    assert "Kırılım tetiği" in tablo[0]


def test_walk_forward_sinyal_gecmisini_haftalik_adimlarla_uretir() -> None:
    veri = tarihsel_veri()
    kesim = str(veri.index[119].date())
    motor = MomentumWalkForwardEngine(
        MomentumDiscoveryEngine(analiz_motoru=SahteAnalizMotoru())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    sonuclar, hatalar = motor.calistir(
        {"AAA.IS": veri},
        kesim_tarihi=kesim,
        sinyal_gecmisi_olustur=True,
        sinyal_gecmisi_adim_sayisi=4,
        sinyal_gecmisi_adim_bari=5,
    )

    assert hatalar == ()
    assert len(sonuclar[0].sinyal_gecmisi) == 4
    tablo = sinyal_gecmisi_tablosu(sonuclar[0])
    assert len(tablo) == 4
    assert "Kırılım tetiği" in tablo[-1]
    assert tablo[-1]["Tarih"] == kesim

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_morning_revalidation_candidate_replacement_v1.py
from types import SimpleNamespace

from backend.app.analysis.daily_buy_decision_closeout import build_daily_buy_decisions
from backend.app.analysis.daily_buy_decision_cycle import (
    build_daily_buy_decision_snapshot,
    compare_evening_morning_replacements,
    daily_buy_decision_replacement_rows,
)


def _card(symbol, rank, *, source="TARAMA", readiness="TEYİT BEKLİYOR", quality="-", score=70):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        rank=rank,
        symbol=symbol,
        technical_score=score,
        technical_view="Nötr Teknik Görünüm",
        research_priority="NORMAL",
        visibility_state="IMPROVING",
        rank_change=10,
        operational_status="TEKNİK ADAY",
        data_blocked=False,
        volume_ratio=1.2,
        risk_reward=1.5,
        selection_source=source,
        jump_quality=quality,
        entry_readiness=readiness,
        risks=(),
    )


def _snapshot(cards, phase):
    decisions = build_daily_buy_decisions(cards)
    return build_daily_buy_decision_snapshot(cards, phase=phase, decisions=decisions)


def test_revalidation_pairs_evening_exit_with_morning_entry():
    evening_cards = (
        _card("AAA", 1),
        _card("BBB", 2),
        _card("CCC", 3),
        _card("DDD", 4),
        _card("EEE", 5),
    )
    morning_cards = (
        _card("AAA", 1),
        _card("BBB", 2),
        _card("CCC", 3),
        _card("NEW", 4, source="SICRAMA", readiness="GİRİŞE HAZIR", quality="UYGUN", score=78),
        _card("DDD", 5),
    )
    replacements = compare_evening_morning_replacements(
        _snapshot(evening_cards, "EVENING"),
        _snapshot(morning_cards, "MORNING"),
    )
    assert len(replacements) == 1
    item = replacements[0]
    assert item.outgoing_symbol == "EEE"
    assert item.incoming_symbol == "NEW"
    assert "sıçrama havuzundan" in item.replacement_context
    assert "Giriş hazırlığı: GİRİŞE HAZIR" in item.replacement_context
    assert "Sıçrama kalite kapısı: UYGUN" in item.replacement_context


def test_revalidation_has_no_replacement_when_membership_is_same():
    cards = tuple(_card(symbol, rank) for rank, symbol in enumerate(("AAA", "BBB", "CCC", "DDD", "EEE"), 1))
    replacements = compare_evening_morning_replacements(
        _snapshot(cards, "EVENING"),
        _snapshot(cards, "MORNING"),
    )
    assert replacements == ()


def test_replacement_rows_expose_outgoing_and_incoming_decisions():
    evening = (_card("OLD", 5),)
    morning = (_card("NEW", 4, source="SICRAMA", readiness="GİRİŞE HAZIR", quality="UYGUN", score=78),)
    rows = daily_buy_decision_replacement_rows(
        compare_evening_morning_replacements(
            _snapshot(evening, "EVENING"),
            _snapshot(morning, "MORNING"),
        )
    )
    assert rows[0]["Listeden çıkan"] == "OLD"
    assert rows[0]["Listeye giren"] == "NEW"
    assert rows[0]["Sabah karar"] in {"AL", "BEKLE", "İŞLEM YOK"}

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_morning_revalidation_decision_change_reason_v1.py
from types import SimpleNamespace

from backend.app.analysis.daily_buy_decision_closeout import build_daily_buy_decisions
from backend.app.analysis.daily_buy_decision_cycle import (
    build_daily_buy_decision_snapshot,
    compare_evening_morning,
    daily_buy_decision_change_rows,
)


def _card(
    *,
    symbol="AAA",
    rank=1,
    score=75,
    view="Pozitif Teknik Görünüm",
    volume=1.20,
    rr=1.80,
    readiness="GİRİŞE HAZIR",
    source="SICRAMA",
    quality="UYGUN",
    rank_change=20,
):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        rank=rank,
        symbol=symbol,
        technical_score=score,
        technical_view=view,
        research_priority="HIGH",
        visibility_state="IMPROVING",
        rank_change=rank_change,
        operational_status="TEKNİK ADAY",
        data_blocked=False,
        volume_ratio=volume,
        risk_reward=rr,
        selection_source=source,
        jump_quality=quality,
        entry_readiness=readiness,
        risks=(),
    )


def _snapshot(card, phase):
    decisions = build_daily_buy_decisions((card,))
    return build_daily_buy_decision_snapshot((card,), phase=phase, decisions=decisions)


def test_morning_revalidation_explains_al_to_bekle_from_volume_loss():
    evening_card = _card(volume=1.20)
    morning_card = _card(volume=0.85)
    change = compare_evening_morning(
        _snapshot(evening_card, "EVENING"),
        _snapshot(morning_card, "MORNING"),
    )[0]
    assert change.evening_decision == "AL"
    assert change.morning_decision == "BEKLE"
    assert "Karar AL→BEKLE" in change.change_reason
    assert "Hacim teyidi kayboldu" in change.change_reason


def test_morning_revalidation_explains_bekle_to_al_from_entry_readiness():
    evening_card = _card(readiness="TEYİT BEKLİYOR")
    morning_card = _card(readiness="GİRİŞE HAZIR")
    change = compare_evening_morning(
        _snapshot(evening_card, "EVENING"),
        _snapshot(morning_card, "MORNING"),
    )[0]
    assert change.evening_decision == "BEKLE"
    assert change.morning_decision == "AL"
    assert "Karar BEKLE→AL" in change.change_reason
    assert "Giriş hazırlığı TEYİT BEKLİYOR→GİRİŞE HAZIR" in change.change_reason


def test_change_rows_show_evening_and_morning_closeout_decision():
    card = _card()
    change = compare_evening_morning(_snapshot(card, "EVENING"), _snapshot(card, "MORNING"))[0]
    row = daily_buy_decision_change_rows((change,))[0]
    assert row["Akşam karar"] == "AL"
    assert row["Sabah karar"] == "AL"

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_next_day_walk_forward.py
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from app.analysis.next_day_outcome import (
    NextDayCandidateOutcome,
    NextDayOutcomeResult,
    NextDayTierOutcomeSummary,
    OUTCOME_CONTRACT_ID,
)
from app.analysis.next_day_selection import NextDayRole, STRATEGY_ID
from app.analysis.next_day_walk_forward import (
    REQUIRED_VALID_SESSIONS,
    WALK_FORWARD_CONTRACT_ID,
    build_walk_forward_readiness,
)


def tier(role: NextDayRole, rows: tuple[NextDayCandidateOutcome, ...]) -> NextDayTierOutcomeSummary:
    selected = tuple(row for row in rows if row.role is role)
    hits = sum(row.status == "HIT" for row in selected)
    misses = sum(row.status == "MISS" for row in selected)
    relatives = [float(row.relative_return) for row in selected if row.relative_return is not None]
    mfes = [float(row.mfe) for row in selected if row.mfe is not None]
    maes = [float(row.mae) for row in selected if row.mae is not None]
    return NextDayTierOutcomeSummary(
        role=role,
        candidate_count=len(selected),
        evaluated_count=len(selected),
        pending_count=0,
        hit_count=hits,
        miss_count=misses,
        hit_rate=(hits / len(selected)) if selected else None,
        median_relative_return=(sorted(relatives)[len(relatives)//2] if relatives else None),
        average_relative_return=(sum(relatives) / len(relatives)) if relatives else None,
        median_mfe=(sorted(mfes)[len(mfes)//2] if mfes else None),
        median_mae=(sorted(maes)[len(maes)//2] if maes else None),
        basket_success=None,
    )


def row(date: str, symbol: str, role: NextDayRole, order: int, ret: float, benchmark: float = 0.01):
    relative = ret - benchmark
    return NextDayCandidateOutcome(
        evaluation_identity=f"{STRATEGY_ID}|{date}|{symbol}",
        canonical_date=date,
        t1_date="2026-09-01",
        symbol=symbol,
        role=role,
        selection_order=order,
        t_close=100.0,
        t1_open=100.0,
        t1_high=106.0,
        t1_low=97.0,
        t1_close=100.0 * (1.0 + ret),
        close_to_close_return=ret,
        relative_return=relative,
        mfe=0.06,
        mae=-0.03,
        benchmark_return=benchmark,
        status="HIT" if ret > benchmark else "MISS",
    )


def session(index: int, *, primary_count: int = 2, reserve_count: int = 2) -> NextDayOutcomeResult:
    date = f"2026-09-{index + 1:02d}"
    rows = []
    for i in range(primary_count):
        ret = 0.04 if i % 2 == 0 else -0.01
        rows.append(row(date, f"P{index:02d}{i}", NextDayRole.PRIMARY, i + 1, ret))
    for i in range(reserve_count):
        ret = 0.03 if i % 2 == 0 else -0.02
        rows.append(row(date, f"R{index:02d}{i}", NextDayRole.RESERVE, i + 1, ret))
    rows_t = tuple(rows)
    p = tier(NextDayRole.PRIMARY, rows_t)
    r = tier(NextDayRole.RESERVE, rows_t)
    spread = None
    if p.median_relative_return is not None and r.median_relative_return is not None:
        spread = p.median_relative_return - r.median_relative_return
    return NextDayOutcomeResult(
        contract_id=OUTCOME_CONTRACT_ID,
        strategy_id=STRATEGY_ID,
        canonical_date=date,
        t1_date="2026-09-01",
        selection_digest=(f"{index + 1:064x}")[-64:],
        benchmark_return=0.01,
        outcomes=rows_t,
        primary=p,
        reserve=r,
        primary_reserve_relative_spread=spread,
    )


def test_collecting_below_20_valid_sessions():
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness(session(i) for i in range(19))
    assert result.contract_id == WALK_FORWARD_CONTRACT_ID
    assert result.required_valid_sessions == REQUIRED_VALID_SESSIONS
    assert result.valid_session_count == 19
    assert result.status == "COLLECTING"
    assert result.ready_for_review is False


def test_ready_at_20_valid_sessions():
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness(session(i) for i in range(20))
    assert result.valid_session_count == 20
    assert result.status == "READY_FOR_REVIEW"
    assert result.ready_for_review is True


def test_no_forced_fill_sparse_or_empty_session_can_still_be_valid():
    sparse = session(0, primary_count=1, reserve_count=0)
    empty = session(1, primary_count=0, reserve_count=0)
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((sparse, empty), required_valid_sessions=2)
    assert result.ready_for_review is True
    assert result.valid_session_count == 2
    assert result.primary.observation_count == 1
    assert result.reserve.observation_count == 0


def test_pending_outcome_excludes_session_fail_closed():
    s = session(0)
    first = replace(s.outcomes[0], status="PENDING", close_to_close_return=None, relative_return=None, mfe=None, mae=None)
    rows = (first,) + s.outcomes[1:]
    s = replace(s, outcomes=rows)
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((s,))
    assert result.valid_session_count == 0
    assert result.invalid_session_count == 1
    assert result.issues[0].reason == "PENDING_OR_INVALID_OUTCOME"


def test_duplicate_canonical_date_is_not_double_counted():
    s = session(0)
    duplicate = replace(s, selection_digest="f" * 64)
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((s, duplicate), required_valid_sessions=1)
    assert result.valid_session_count == 0
    assert result.invalid_session_count == 2
    assert {issue.reason for issue in result.issues} == {"DUPLICATE_CANONICAL_DATE"}


def test_invalid_digest_excludes_session():
    s = replace(session(0), selection_digest="bad")
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((s,))
    assert result.issues[0].reason == "SELECTION_DIGEST_INVALID"


def test_contract_or_strategy_mismatch_excludes_session():
    a = replace(session(0), contract_id="OTHER")
    b = replace(session(1), strategy_id="OTHER")
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((a, b))
    assert {issue.reason for issue in result.issues} == {"OUTCOME_CONTRACT_MISMATCH", "STRATEGY_MISMATCH"}


def test_aggregate_metrics_keep_primary_and_reserve_separate():
    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((session(0),))
    assert result.primary.observation_count == 2
    assert result.reserve.observation_count == 2
    assert result.primary.hit_rate == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert result.reserve.hit_rate == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert result.primary.average_return == pytest.approx(0.015)
    assert result.reserve.average_return == pytest.approx(0.005)
    assert result.primary.average_relative_return == pytest.approx(0.005)
    assert result.reserve.average_relative_return == pytest.approx(-0.005)
    assert result.primary_reserve_median_relative_spread is not None


def test_three_of_five_counter_only_uses_full_primary_five_sessions():
    full = session(0, primary_count=5, reserve_count=0)
    sparse = session(1, primary_count=3, reserve_count=0)

    assert result.primary_three_of_five_hit_session_count == 1

    result = build_walk_forward_readiness((session(0),))
    assert result.primary_average_above_benchmark_session_count == 1
    assert result.primary_average_negative_return_session_count == 0

    sessions = [session(2), session(0), session(1)]

    assert result.valid_canonical_dates == ("2026-09-01", "2026-09-02", "2026-09-03")

    assert result.outcome_tuning is False
    assert result.new_score is False
    assert result.new_store is False
    assert result.new_scanner is False
    assert result.al_sat_authority is False

def test_monthly_profile_status_precedence() -> None:
    current = {
        "snapshot": "CURRENT_PARTIAL",
        "matches": [{"quality": 19.59, "profile": "P1", "excess3": 3.37, "excess6": 14.86}],
    }
    closed = {
        "snapshot": "LAST_CLOSED",
        "matches": [{"quality": 14.68, "profile": "P2", "excess3": 5.69, "excess6": 6.92}],
    }
    status = radar._monthly_profile_status(current, closed)
    assert status["Durum"] == "Ay İçi Geçici Match"
    assert status["Profile Match Count"] == 1

    status = radar._monthly_profile_status(
        {"snapshot": "CURRENT_PARTIAL", "matches": []},
        closed,
    )
    assert status["Durum"] == "Validated Profile Match"
    assert status["Profile Match Count"] == 1


def test_monthly_profile_no_match_contract() -> None:
    status = radar._monthly_profile_status(
        {"snapshot": "CURRENT_PARTIAL", "matches": []},
        {"snapshot": "LAST_CLOSED", "matches": []},
    )
    assert status["Durum"] == "Henüz Validate Değil"
    assert status["Profile Match Count"] == 0

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from backend.app.backtest.backtest_models import (
    BacktestTrade,
    CIKIS_SURE_SONU,
)
from backend.app.backtest.portfolio_backtest import (
    PORTFOY_KAPASITE_NEDENI,
    PORTFOY_SEMBOL_CAKISMA_NEDENI,
    PortfolioBacktestAnalyzer,
    PortfolioBacktestConfig,
    PortfolioBacktestError,
)


def fiyat_verisi(
    kapanislar: list[float],
    baslangic: str = "2026-01-01",
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    tarihler = pd.date_range(
        baslangic,
        periods=len(kapanislar),
        freq="D",
    )
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "Open": kapanislar,
            "Close": kapanislar,
        },
        index=tarihler,
    )


def islem(
    sembol: str,
    giris_gunu: int,
    cikis_gunu: int,
    giris: float,
    cikis: float,
    skor: int = 60,
) -> BacktestTrade:
    baslangic = date(2026, 1, 1)
    sinyal_tarihi = pd.Timestamp(baslangic) + pd.Timedelta(
        days=giris_gunu - 1
    )
    giris_tarihi = pd.Timestamp(baslangic) + pd.Timedelta(
        days=giris_gunu
    )
    cikis_tarihi = pd.Timestamp(baslangic) + pd.Timedelta(
        days=cikis_gunu
    )
    return BacktestTrade.olustur(
        sembol=sembol,
        sinyal_tarihi=sinyal_tarihi.date(),
        giris_tarihi=giris_tarihi.date(),
        cikis_tarihi=cikis_tarihi.date(),
        giris_fiyati=giris,
        cikis_fiyati=cikis,
        skor=skor,
        teknik_gorunum="Pozitif Teknik Görünüm",
        elde_tutulan_bar=max(1, cikis_gunu - giris_gunu + 1),
        cikis_nedeni=CIKIS_SURE_SONU,
        islem_maliyeti_orani=0.0,
    )


def test_ayni_gun_kapasitede_yuksek_skor_oncelik_alir() -> None:
    veriler = {
        "AAA.IS": fiyat_verisi([100, 100, 110]),
        "BBB.IS": fiyat_verisi([100, 100, 105]),
        "CCC.IS": fiyat_verisi([100, 100, 120]),
    }
    islemler = [
        islem("AAA.IS", 1, 2, 100, 110, skor=70),
        islem("BBB.IS", 1, 2, 100, 105, skor=60),
        islem("CCC.IS", 1, 2, 100, 120, skor=80),
    ]

    rapor = PortfolioBacktestAnalyzer().analiz_et(
        fiyat_verileri=veriler,
        islemler=islemler,
        yapilandirma=PortfolioBacktestConfig(
            baslangic_sermayesi=100_000,
            maksimum_es_zamanli_pozisyon=2,
            islem_maliyeti_orani=0.0,
        ),
    )

    assert [
        kayit.islem.sembol
        for kayit in rapor.kabul_edilen_islemler
    ] == ["CCC.IS", "AAA.IS"]
    assert rapor.atlanan_islem_sayisi == 1
    assert rapor.atlanan_islemler[0].neden == PORTFOY_KAPASITE_NEDENI


def test_cikis_gunu_sermaye_yeni_giriste_tekrar_kullanilmaz() -> None:
    veriler = {
        "AAA.IS": fiyat_verisi([100, 100, 110, 110]),
        "BBB.IS": fiyat_verisi([100, 100, 100, 110]),
    }
    islemler = [
        islem("AAA.IS", 1, 2, 100, 110, skor=80),
        islem("BBB.IS", 2, 3, 100, 110, skor=90),
    ]

    rapor = PortfolioBacktestAnalyzer().analiz_et(
        fiyat_verileri=veriler,
        islemler=islemler,
        yapilandirma=PortfolioBacktestConfig(
            maksimum_es_zamanli_pozisyon=1,
            islem_maliyeti_orani=0.0,
        ),
    )

    assert rapor.tamamlanan_islem_sayisi == 1
    assert rapor.atlanan_islem_sayisi == 1
    assert rapor.atlanan_islemler[0].neden == PORTFOY_KAPASITE_NEDENI


def test_portfoy_kaldirac_kullanmadan_esit_slot_ayirir() -> None:
    veriler = {

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_backtest.py
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_backtest import (
    PreBreakoutReplayError,
    replay_ozeti,
    tarihsel_yeniden_oynat,
)


def tarihsel_veri(gun: int = 120) -> pd.DataFrame:
    index = pd.bdate_range("2026-01-02", periods=gun)
    close = np.linspace(100.0, 118.0, gun)
    close += np.sin(np.linspace(0.0, 10.0, gun))
    spread = np.linspace(2.0, 1.0, gun)
    volume = np.full(gun, 2_000_000.0)
    volume[-15:] = 2_300_000.0

    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "Open": close - 0.20,
            "High": close + spread / 2,
            "Low": close - spread / 2,
            "Close": close,
            "Volume": volume,
        },
        index=index,
    )


def test_yeniden_oynatma_kesim_sonrasi_veriyi_analizde_kullanmaz() -> None:
    data = tarihsel_veri()
    cutoff = data.index[89].date()

    result = tarihsel_yeniden_oynat(
        "TEST.IS",
        data,
        cutoff,
    )

    assert result.cutoff_date == cutoff
    assert result.entry_close == pytest.approx(float(data.iloc[89]["Close"]))
    assert result.analysis.features is not None
    assert result.analysis.features.close == pytest.approx(
        float(data.iloc[89]["Close"])
    )


def test_ileri_performans_1_3_5_10_20_gun_uretilir() -> None:
    data = tarihsel_veri()
    cutoff = data.index[89].date()

    result = tarihsel_yeniden_oynat("TEST.IS", data, cutoff)

    assert [item.horizon for item in result.forward] == [1, 3, 5, 10, 20]
    assert all(item.available for item in result.forward)


def test_ileri_performans_yetersizse_mevcut_degil_isaretlenir() -> None:
    data = tarihsel_veri(100)
    cutoff = data.index[94].date()

    result = tarihsel_yeniden_oynat("TEST.IS", data, cutoff)

    twenty_day = next(item for item in result.forward if item.horizon == 20)
    assert twenty_day.available is False
    assert twenty_day.close_return_pct is None


def test_bos_veri_reddedilir() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(PreBreakoutReplayError):
        tarihsel_yeniden_oynat(
            "TEST.IS",
            pd.DataFrame(),
            date(2026, 7, 1),
        )


def test_gecersiz_ufuk_reddedilir() -> None:
    data = tarihsel_veri()
    cutoff = data.index[89].date()

    with pytest.raises(PreBreakoutReplayError):
        tarihsel_yeniden_oynat(
            "TEST.IS",
            data,
            cutoff,
            horizons=(0,),
        )


def test_ozet_bos_listeyi_guvenli_isler() -> None:
    summary = replay_ozeti([])

    assert summary.total_replays == 0
    assert summary.accepted_candidates == 0
    assert summary.positive_5d_rate_pct == 0.0


def test_ozet_sayimlari_tutarlidir() -> None:
    data = tarihsel_veri()
    cutoffs = [
        data.index[80].date(),
        data.index[85].date(),
        data.index[90].date(),
    ]

    results = [
        tarihsel_yeniden_oynat("TEST.IS", data, cutoff)
        for cutoff in cutoffs
    ]
    summary = replay_ozeti(results)

    assert summary.total_replays == 3
    assert (
        summary.accepted_candidates
        + summary.late_entry_count
        + summary.rejected_count
        == 3
    )

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_calibration import (
    ThresholdCalibrationConfig,
    compare_thresholds,
    generate_calibration_report,
)


def records_table() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "score": [35, 40, 44, 45, 46, 49, 50, 52, 55, 58, 60, 65],
            "performance_label": [
                "Yanlış pozitif",
                "Sınırlı hareket",
                "Yanlış pozitif",
                "Yanlış pozitif",
                "Sınırlı hareket",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
            ],
        }
    )


def test_esikler_deterministik_ve_mevcut_esikle_birlikte_karsilastirilir() -> None:
    config = ThresholdCalibrationConfig(
        current_threshold=45,
        alternative_thresholds=(55, 40, 50, 40),
        minimum_sample_size=2,
    )

    result = compare_thresholds(records_table(), config)

    assert result["threshold"].tolist() == [40, 45, 50, 55]
    assert result["is_current"].sum() == 1
    assert result.loc[result["threshold"] == 50, "sample_count"].iloc[0] == 6


def test_rapor_uygun_alternatifi_onerir_ama_otomatik_degistirmez() -> None:
    config = ThresholdCalibrationConfig(
        current_threshold=45,
        alternative_thresholds=(50, 55),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
        minimum_coverage_pct=50.0,
        minimum_quality_gain=3.0,
    )

    report = generate_calibration_report(records_table(), config)

    assert report.recommendation.recommended_threshold in (50, 55)
    assert report.recommendation.automatic_change_applied is False
    assert "otomatik" in report.recommendation.reason.lower()


def test_yetersiz_orneklemde_mevcut_esik_korunur() -> None:
    config = ThresholdCalibrationConfig(
        current_threshold=45,
        alternative_thresholds=(50,),
        minimum_sample_size=100,
    )

    report = generate_calibration_report(records_table(), config)

    assert report.recommendation.recommended_threshold == 45
    assert report.recommendation.decision == "Yetersiz örneklem"


def test_bos_veri_guvenli_rapor_uretir() -> None:
    report = generate_calibration_report(
        pd.DataFrame(),
        ThresholdCalibrationConfig(),
    )

    assert report.comparison.empty
    assert report.recommendation.decision == "Yetersiz veri"


def test_eksik_sutun_reddedilir() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="performance_label"):
        compare_thresholds(
            pd.DataFrame({"score": [45]}),
            ThresholdCalibrationConfig(),
        )


def test_gecersiz_esik_reddedilir() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ThresholdCalibrationConfig(current_threshold=101)


FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_calibration_confidence.py
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_calibration import (
    PreBreakoutCalibrationRow,
    guvenilir_asama_satirlarini_sec,
    kalibrasyon_ozetini_hesapla,
)


def row(
    stage: str,
    *,
    candidates: int,
    measured: int,
    success_rate: float,
) -> PreBreakoutCalibrationRow:
    return PreBreakoutCalibrationRow(
        stage=stage,
        candidate_count=candidates,
        measured_count=measured,
        successful_count=round(measured * success_rate / 100),
        limited_count=0,
        false_positive_count=0,
        success_rate_pct=success_rate,
        average_close_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_20d_pct=None,
    )


def test_olcum_kapsami_toplam_aday_uzerinden_hesaplanir() -> None:
    summary = kalibrasyon_ozetini_hesapla(
        (
            row("E2", candidates=4, measured=3, success_rate=50.0),
            row("E3", candidates=6, measured=5, success_rate=60.0),
        ),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
    )

    assert summary.candidate_count == 10
    assert summary.measured_count == 8
    assert summary.measurement_coverage_pct == 80.0


def test_en_iyi_asama_yalnizca_yeterli_orneklemden_secilir() -> None:
    summary = kalibrasyon_ozetini_hesapla(
        (
            row("E2", candidates=2, measured=2, success_rate=100.0),
            row("E3", candidates=5, measured=5, success_rate=60.0),
        ),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
    )

    assert summary.best_stage == "E3"
    assert summary.best_stage_success_rate_pct == 60.0
    assert summary.reliable_stage_count == 1


def test_esit_basarida_daha_fazla_olculen_asama_onceliklidir() -> None:

        (
            row("E2", candidates=3, measured=3, success_rate=50.0),

def test_yetersiz_orneklemde_en_iyi_asama_uretilmez() -> None:
    summary = kalibrasyon_ozetini_hesapla(
        (row("E3", candidates=2, measured=2, success_rate=100.0),),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
    )

    assert summary.best_stage is None
    assert summary.sufficient_sample is False
    assert "örneklem yetersiz" in summary.note


def test_olcum_yokken_acik_uyari_uretilir() -> None:

    )

    except ValueError as error:

    else:
        raise AssertionError("ValueError bekleniyordu")

from pathlib import Path


UI = Path("backend/app/ui/prebreakout_validation_dashboard.py")


def test_toplu_dogrulama_ekrani_kalibrasyon_raporunu_kullanir() -> None:
    source = UI.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")

    assert "generate_calibration_report" in source
    assert 'st.subheader("Eşik kalibrasyon önerisi")' in source
    assert "automatic_change_applied" in source
    assert "Kalibrasyon önerisini CSV indir" in source


def test_ekran_uretim_esigini_yazmaz() -> None:
    source = UI.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")

    forbidden = (
        "write_config(",
        "save_config(",
        "set_production_threshold(",
    )
    assert all(item not in source for item in forbidden)


FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_calibration_intervals.py
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_calibration import (
    PreBreakoutCalibrationRow,
    _wilson_interval,
    asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla,
    asama_guven_araliklarini_tabloya_cevir,
    temkinli_lider_asamayi_sec,
)


def row(stage: str, measured: int, successful: int) -> PreBreakoutCalibrationRow:
    return PreBreakoutCalibrationRow(
        stage=stage,
        candidate_count=measured,
        measured_count=measured,
        successful_count=successful,
        limited_count=0,
        false_positive_count=measured-successful,
        success_rate_pct=round(successful / measured * 100, 2) if measured else 0.0,
        average_close_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_20d_pct=None,
    )


def test_wilson_araligi_bos_orneklemde_sifirdir() -> None:
    assert _wilson_interval(0, 0) == (0.0, 0.0)


def test_wilson_araligi_gecersiz_sayilari_reddeder() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        _wilson_interval(4, 3)


def test_guven_araligi_yuzde_sinirlarinda_kalir() -> None:
    result = asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla((row("E3", 10, 6),))[0]
    assert 0.0 <= result.confidence_low_pct <= 60.0
    assert 60.0 <= result.confidence_high_pct <= 100.0


def test_yetersiz_ornekleme_temkinli_sira_verilmez() -> None:
    result = asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla(
        (row("E4", 2, 2),), minimum_sample_size=3
    )[0]
    assert result.sample_sufficient is False
    assert result.conservative_rank is None


def test_temkinli_sira_sadece_ham_basari_oranina_bakmaz() -> None:
    results = asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla(
        (row("E2", 3, 3), row("E3", 20, 14)), minimum_sample_size=3
    )
    leader = temkinli_lider_asamayi_sec(results)
    assert leader is not None
    assert leader.stage == "E3"


def test_esit_alt_sinirda_daha_yuksek_basarili_oran_onceliklidir() -> None:
    results = asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla(
        (row("E2", 10, 5), row("E3", 10, 5)), minimum_sample_size=3
    )
    assert sorted(item.conservative_rank for item in results) == [1, 2]


def test_temkinli_lider_yoksa_none_doner() -> None:
    results = asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla(
        (row("E1", 1, 1),), minimum_sample_size=3
    )
    assert temkinli_lider_asamayi_sec(results) is None


def test_guven_araligi_tablosu_karar_sutunlarini_icerir() -> None:
    rows = asama_guven_araliklarini_hesapla((row("E3", 10, 6),))
    table = asama_guven_araliklarini_tabloya_cevir(rows)
    assert "%95 alt sınır" in table.columns
    assert "Temkinli sıra" in table.columns
    assert table.iloc[0]["Örneklem yeterli"] == "Evet"

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_calibration import (
    PreBreakoutCalibrationRow,
    lider_duyarlilik_senaryolarini_hesapla,
    lider_duyarlilik_senaryolarini_tabloya_cevir,
    lider_kararlilik_ozetini_hesapla,
)


def row(stage: str, measured: int, successful: int) -> PreBreakoutCalibrationRow:
    return PreBreakoutCalibrationRow(
        stage=stage,
        candidate_count=measured,
        measured_count=measured,
        successful_count=successful,
        limited_count=0,
        false_positive_count=measured-successful,
        success_rate_pct=round(successful / measured * 100, 2) if measured else 0.0,
        average_close_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_20d_pct=None,
    )


def test_kararli_lider_tek_gozlem_senaryolarinda_korunur() -> None:
    rows = (row("E2", 12, 5), row("E3", 20, 16))
    summary = lider_kararlilik_ozetini_hesapla(rows, minimum_sample_size=3)
    assert summary.baseline_leader == "E3"
    assert summary.scenario_count == 4
    assert summary.retention_rate_pct >= 80.0
    assert summary.stable is True


def test_kucuk_orneklemli_lider_kirilgan_olabilir() -> None:
    rows = (row("E2", 3, 3), row("E3", 10, 7))
    summary = lider_kararlilik_ozetini_hesapla(rows, minimum_sample_size=3)
    assert summary.baseline_leader == "E2"
    assert summary.scenario_count == 3
    assert summary.stable is False
    assert "kırılgan" in summary.note


def test_lider_yoksa_kararlilik_olculemez() -> None:
    summary = lider_kararlilik_ozetini_hesapla(
        (row("E1", 2, 2),), minimum_sample_size=3
    )
    assert summary.baseline_leader is None
    assert summary.stable is False
    assert "ölçülemedi" in summary.note


def test_duyarlilik_senaryolari_basarili_ve_basarisiz_gozlemi_ayirir() -> None:
    scenarios = lider_duyarlilik_senaryolarini_hesapla(
        (row("E2", 5, 3), row("E3", 6, 4)), minimum_sample_size=3
    )
    assert len(scenarios) == 4
    assert {item.removed_outcome for item in scenarios} == {
        "Başarılı gözlem",
        "Başarılı olmayan gözlem",
    }


def test_duyarlilik_tablosu_karar_sutunlarini_icerir() -> None:
    scenarios = lider_duyarlilik_senaryolarini_hesapla(
        (row("E2", 5, 3), row("E3", 6, 4)), minimum_sample_size=3
    )
    table = lider_duyarlilik_senaryolarini_tabloya_cevir(scenarios)
    assert "Senaryo lideri" in table.columns
    assert "Mevcut lider korundu" in table.columns

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_calibration_threshold_recommendations.py
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_calibration import (
    PreBreakoutCalibrationRow,
    PreBreakoutMomentumCalibrationRow,
    kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla,
    kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_tabloya_cevir,
)


def stage_row(stage: str, measured: int, successful: int) -> PreBreakoutCalibrationRow:
    return PreBreakoutCalibrationRow(
        stage=stage,
        candidate_count=measured,
        measured_count=measured,
        successful_count=successful,
        limited_count=0,
        false_positive_count=measured - successful,
        success_rate_pct=round(successful / measured * 100, 2) if measured else 0.0,
        average_close_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_close_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_max_return_20d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_5d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_10d_pct=None,
        average_min_return_20d_pct=None,
    )


def momentum_row(stage: str, band: str, measured: int, successful: int) -> PreBreakoutMomentumCalibrationRow:
    return PreBreakoutMomentumCalibrationRow(
        stage=stage,
        momentum_band=band,
        candidate_count=measured,
        measured_count=measured,
        successful_count=successful,
        success_rate_pct=round(successful / measured * 100, 2) if measured else 0.0,
        average_macd_histogram_slope=None,
        average_rsi_slope=None,
    )


def test_kararli_temkinli_lider_kontrollu_dogrulama_adayi_olur() -> None:
    recommendations = kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla(
        (stage_row("E2", 12, 5), stage_row("E3", 20, 16)),
        (
            momentum_row("E2", "10-14", 12, 5),
            momentum_row("E3", "10-14", 8, 5),
            momentum_row("E3", "15-20", 12, 11),
        ),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
    )
    e3 = next(item for item in recommendations if item.stage == "E3")
    assert e3.recommended_action == "Kontrollü doğrulama adayı"
    assert e3.recommended_minimum_momentum_score == 15
    assert e3.leader_stable is True
    assert e3.auto_apply is False


def test_yetersiz_orneklem_esik_onerisi_uretmez() -> None:
    recommendations = kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla(
        (stage_row("E2", 2, 2),),
        (momentum_row("E2", "15-20", 2, 2),),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
    )
    assert recommendations[0].recommended_action == "Veri biriktir"
    assert recommendations[0].recommended_minimum_momentum_score is None
    assert recommendations[0].evidence_level == "Yetersiz"


def test_zayif_alt_guven_siniri_gevsetme_onermez() -> None:
    recommendations = kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla(
        (stage_row("E1", 10, 1),),
        (momentum_row("E1", "0-9", 10, 1),),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
        minimum_confidence_low_pct=20.0,
    )
    assert recommendations[0].recommended_action == "Gevşetme önerilmez"


def test_esik_onerileri_uretim_motoruna_otomatik_uygulanmaz() -> None:
    recommendations = kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla(
        (stage_row("E3", 20, 16),),
        (momentum_row("E3", "15-20", 20, 16),),
    )
    assert all(item.auto_apply is False for item in recommendations)


def test_esik_onerisi_parametre_dogrulamasi() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla((), (), minimum_sample_size=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla(
            (), (), minimum_confidence_low_pct=101.0
        )


def test_esik_onerisi_tablosu_karar_sutunlarini_icerir() -> None:
    recommendations = kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_hesapla(
        (stage_row("E3", 20, 16),),
        (momentum_row("E3", "15-20", 20, 16),),
    )
    table = kalibrasyon_esik_onerilerini_tabloya_cevir(recommendations)
    assert "Önerilen minimum momentum" in table.columns
    assert "Otomatik uygulanır" in table.columns
    assert set(table["Otomatik uygulanır"]) == {"Hayır"}

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_calibration_threshold_validation.py
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_calibration import (
    PreBreakoutMomentumCalibrationRow,
    PreBreakoutThresholdRecommendation,
    esik_dogrulamalarini_tabloya_cevir,
    esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula,
)


def momentum(band: str, measured: int, successful: int):
    return PreBreakoutMomentumCalibrationRow(
        stage="E3", momentum_band=band, candidate_count=measured,
        measured_count=measured, successful_count=successful,
        success_rate_pct=round(successful / measured * 100, 2),
        average_macd_histogram_slope=None, average_rsi_slope=None,
    )


def recommendation(action="Kontrollü doğrulama adayı", score=15):
    return PreBreakoutThresholdRecommendation(
        stage="E3", recommended_action=action,
        recommended_minimum_momentum_score=score, evidence_level="Güçlü",
        confidence_low_pct=40.0, measured_count=20, leader_stable=True,
        auto_apply=False, rationale="test",
    )


def test_onerilen_esik_mevcut_esikle_ayni_orneklemde_karsilastirilir():
    rows = esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula(
        (recommendation(),), (momentum("10-14", 8, 4), momentum("15-20", 12, 10))
    )
    item = rows[0]
    assert item.baseline_measured_count == 20
    assert item.candidate_measured_count == 12
    assert item.candidate_success_rate_pct > item.baseline_success_rate_pct
    assert item.verdict == "Kontrollü uygulama adayı"
    assert item.auto_apply is False


def test_asiri_orneklem_kaybi_adayi_reddeder():
    rows = esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula(
        (recommendation(),), (momentum("10-14", 18, 8), momentum("15-20", 2, 2)),
        minimum_candidate_sample_size=2,
    )
    assert rows[0].verdict == "Aşırı örneklem kaybı"


def test_yetersiz_aday_orneklemi_uygulanmaz():
    rows = esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula(
        (recommendation(),), (momentum("10-14", 8, 4), momentum("15-20", 2, 2))
    )
    assert rows[0].verdict == "Örneklem yetersiz"


def test_kontrollu_dogrulama_adayi_olmayan_oneri_atlanir():
    rows = esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula(
        (recommendation(action="Mevcut eşiği koru"),), (momentum("15-20", 10, 8),)
    )
    assert rows == ()


def test_parametre_dogrulamasi():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula((), (), minimum_candidate_sample_size=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula((), (), minimum_sample_retention_pct=101)


def test_dogrulama_tablosu_karar_ve_guven_sutunlarini_icerir():
    rows = esik_onerilerini_tarihsel_dogrula(
        (recommendation(),), (momentum("10-14", 8, 4), momentum("15-20", 12, 10))
    )
    table = esik_dogrulamalarini_tabloya_cevir(rows)
    assert "Örneklem korunma %" in table.columns
    assert "Öneri %95 alt sınır" in table.columns
    assert set(table["Otomatik uygulanır"]) == {"Hayır"}

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_controlled_calibration.py
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_controlled_calibration import (
    RegimeCalibrationSample,
    ThresholdCalibrationScenario,
    kalibrasyon_degerlendirme_tablosu,
    kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir,
)


def _scenario(
    name: str,
    *,
    sample: int = 100,
    success: int = 55,
    fp: int = 25,
    missed: int = 10,
    regimes: tuple[RegimeCalibrationSample, ...] | None = None,
):
    if regimes is None:
        regimes = (
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Yükselen", 40, 24),
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Yatay", 35, 18),
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Zayıf", 25, 13),
        )
    return ThresholdCalibrationScenario(
        name=name,
        momentum_min=10.0,
        require_positive_rsi_slope=True,
        require_positive_macd_histogram_slope=True,
        sample_count=sample,
        successful_count=success,
        false_positive_count=fp,
        missed_opportunity_count=missed,
        regimes=regimes,
    )


def test_guclu_aday_kontrollu_kapilari_gecer():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario(
        "Kalibre-1",
        sample=90,
        success=56,
        fp=17,
        missed=11,
        regimes=(
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Yükselen", 35, 23),
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Yatay", 30, 18),
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Zayıf", 25, 15),
        ),
    )
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    assert report.recommended_scenario_name == "Kalibre-1"
    assert report.keep_baseline is False
    assert report.automatic_apply_allowed is False


def test_kucuk_orneklem_yuksek_basariya_ragmen_reddedilir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario("Aşırı uyum", sample=20, success=18, fp=1)
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    row = report.rows[0]
    assert row.eligible is False
    assert "Toplam örneklem yetersiz" in row.rejection_reasons
    assert "Örneklem kaybı fazla" in row.rejection_reasons


def test_rejim_cesitliligi_yetersizse_aday_reddedilir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario(
        "Tek rejim",
        sample=80,
        success=52,
        fp=15,
        regimes=(RegimeCalibrationSample("Yükselen", 80, 52),),
    )
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    assert "Rejim çeşitliliği yetersiz" in report.rows[0].rejection_reasons


def test_en_zayif_rejim_fazla_bozulursa_aday_reddedilir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario(
        "Rejim bozulması",
        sample=90,
        success=58,
        fp=15,
        regimes=(
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Yükselen", 40, 30),
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Yatay", 30, 20),
            RegimeCalibrationSample("Zayıf", 20, 8),
        ),
    )
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    assert "En zayıf rejimde bozulma fazla" in report.rows[0].rejection_reasons


def test_yanlis_pozitif_kotulesirse_basari_artsa_da_reddedilir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario("FP kötü", sample=100, success=62, fp=30)
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    assert "Yanlış pozitif oranı kötüleşiyor" in report.rows[0].rejection_reasons


def test_hicbir_aday_gecmezse_mevcut_esikler_korunur():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario("Zayıf aday", sample=95, success=53, fp=24)
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    assert report.keep_baseline is True
    assert report.recommended_scenario_name is None
    assert "mevcut eşikler korunmalı" in report.note


def test_birden_fazla_uygun_adayda_en_guclu_skor_secilir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    a = _scenario("A", sample=90, success=55, fp=18, missed=11)
    b = _scenario("B", sample=92, success=59, fp=16, missed=11)
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (a, b))
    assert report.recommended_scenario_name == "B"


def test_degerlendirme_tablosu_karar_alanlarini_icerir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    candidate = _scenario("A", sample=90, success=56, fp=17)
    report = kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (candidate,))
    table = kalibrasyon_degerlendirme_tablosu(report)
    assert {"Senaryo", "Başarı farkı (puan)", "Örneklem korunumu (%)", "Uygun aday", "Red nedenleri"} <= set(table[0])


def test_gecersiz_senaryo_sayimlari_reddedilir():
    baseline = _scenario("Mevcut")
    bad = _scenario("Bozuk", sample=10, success=11)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(baseline, (bad,))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "kwargs",
    [
        {"minimum_total_sample": 0},
        {"minimum_regime_sample": 0},
        {"minimum_reliable_regime_count": 0},
        {"minimum_sample_retention_pct": 0},
        {"minimum_sample_retention_pct": 101},
        {"minimum_success_improvement_pp": -1},
        {"maximum_false_positive_increase_pp": -1},
        {"maximum_worst_regime_degradation_pp": -1},
    ],
)
def test_gecersiz_kalibrasyon_parametreleri_reddedilir(kwargs):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        kontrollu_esik_kalibrasyonunu_degerlendir(_scenario("Mevcut"), (), **kwargs)

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_historical_validation.py
from datetime import date

import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_historical_validation import (
    HistoricalCandidateObservation,
    build_historical_validation_report,
    captured_move_ratio_pct,
    first_appearance_records,
    forward_return_pct,
    momentum_band_label,
    rank_band_label,
)


def obs(day, symbol, rank, stage, momentum, upside, adverse, price=100):
    return HistoricalCandidateObservation(
        cut_date=date(2026, 7, day),
        symbol=symbol,
        rank=rank,
        stage=stage,
        momentum=momentum,
        signal_price=price,
        future_max_upside_pct=upside,
        max_adverse_excursion_pct=adverse,
    )


def test_rank_bantlari():
    assert rank_band_label(1) == "1-10"
    assert rank_band_label(10) == "1-10"
    assert rank_band_label(11) == "11-20"
    assert rank_band_label(20) == "11-20"
    assert rank_band_label(21) == "21-30"
    assert rank_band_label(30) == "21-30"
    assert rank_band_label(31) == "31+"
    assert rank_band_label(None) == "İzleme dışı"


def test_momentum_bantlari():
    assert momentum_band_label(44.9) == "<45"
    assert momentum_band_label(45) == "45-54"
    assert momentum_band_label(55) == "55-64"
    assert momentum_band_label(65) == "65-74"
    assert momentum_band_label(75) == "75+"
    assert momentum_band_label(None) == "Momentum yok"


def test_rank_bandi_hit_rate_hesabi():
    rows = (
        obs(1, "AAA", 12, "E1", 60, 15, 3),
        obs(1, "BBB", 18, "E2", 63, 5, 4),
        obs(1, "CCC", 25, "E0", 52, 20, 6),
    )
    report = build_historical_validation_report(rows, success_threshold_pct=10)
    b1120 = next(x for x in report.rank_bands if x.label == "11-20")
    assert b1120.observations == 2
    assert b1120.success_count == 1
    assert b1120.hit_rate_pct == pytest.approx(50.0)


def test_stage_istatistikleri():
    rows = (
        obs(1, "AAA", 12, "E1", 60, 15, 3),
        obs(2, "BBB", 14, "E1", 62, 12, 5),
        obs(3, "CCC", 5, "B", 80, 2, 8),
    )
    report = build_historical_validation_report(rows)
    e1 = next(x for x in report.stage_stats if x.label == "E1")
    assert e1.observations == 2
    assert e1.success_count == 2
    assert e1.hit_rate_pct == pytest.approx(100.0)


def test_momentum_istatistikleri():
    rows = (
        obs(1, "AAA", 12, "E1", 60, 15, 3),
        obs(2, "BBB", 20, "E2", 63, 12, 5),
        obs(3, "CCC", 5, "B", 80, 2, 8),
    )
    report = build_historical_validation_report(rows)
    m5564 = next(x for x in report.momentum_bands if x.label == "55-64")
    assert m5564.observations == 2
    assert m5564.hit_rate_pct == pytest.approx(100.0)


def test_olculemeyen_ileri_getiri_hit_rate_disinda_kalir():
    rows = (
        obs(1, "AAA", 12, "E1", 60, None, 3),
        obs(2, "BBB", 18, "E1", 61, 12, 4),
    )
    report = build_historical_validation_report(rows)
    b = next(x for x in report.rank_bands if x.label == "11-20")
    assert b.observations == 2
    assert b.success_count == 1
    assert b.hit_rate_pct == pytest.approx(100.0)


def test_ilk_gorunme_tek_hisseyi_bir_kez_sayar():
    rows = (
        obs(5, "TRMET", 25, "E0", 50, 18, 4),
        obs(6, "TRMET", 18, "E1", 58, 14, 5),
        obs(7, "TRMET", 5, "B", 80, 3, 9),
    )
    first = first_appearance_records(rows)
    assert len(first) == 1
    assert first[0].first_cut_date == date(2026, 7, 5)
    assert first[0].first_rank == 25
    assert first[0].best_future_upside_pct == pytest.approx(18.0)


def test_rapor_raw_ve_unique_symbol_sayisini_ayirir():
    rows = (
        obs(1, "AAA", 20, "E1", 60, 15, 3),
        obs(2, "AAA", 10, "E2", 70, 9, 4),
        obs(1, "BBB", 22, "E0", 55, 11, 5),
    )
    report = build_historical_validation_report(rows)
    assert report.total_observations == 3
    assert report.unique_symbols == 2
    assert len(report.first_appearances) == 2


def test_yakalanan_hareket_orani():
    # 100 -> 130 toplam hareket. 120 seviyesinde ilk 5'e girerse
    # hareketin 20/30'u, yani %66.67'si zaten gerçekleşmiştir.
    ratio = captured_move_ratio_pct(
        move_start_price=100,
        rank_entry_price=120,
        peak_price=130,
    )
    assert ratio == pytest.approx(66.6666667)


def test_hareket_yoksa_yakalanan_oran_none():
    assert captured_move_ratio_pct(
        move_start_price=100,
        rank_entry_price=100,
        peak_price=100,
    ) is None


def test_ileri_getiri():
    assert forward_return_pct(100, 110) == pytest.approx(10.0)


def test_esik_pozitif_olmali():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        build_historical_validation_report((), success_threshold_pct=0)


def test_gecersiz_rank_reddedilir():

def test_gecersiz_fiyat_reddedilir():

            signal_price=0,
            future_max_upside_pct=10,
            max_adverse_excursion_pct=2,
        )

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_score_threshold_calibration.py
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_score_threshold_calibration import (
    ThresholdCalibrationConfig,
    compare_thresholds,
    generate_calibration_report,
)


def records_table() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "score": [35, 40, 44, 45, 46, 49, 50, 52, 55, 58, 60, 65],
            "performance_label": [
                "Yanlış pozitif",
                "Sınırlı hareket",
                "Yanlış pozitif",
                "Yanlış pozitif",
                "Sınırlı hareket",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
                "Başarılı",
            ],
        }
    )


def test_esikler_deterministik_ve_mevcut_esikle_birlikte_karsilastirilir() -> None:
    config = ThresholdCalibrationConfig(
        current_threshold=45,
        alternative_thresholds=(55, 40, 50, 40),
        minimum_sample_size=2,
    )

    result = compare_thresholds(records_table(), config)

    assert result["threshold"].tolist() == [40, 45, 50, 55]
    assert result["is_current"].sum() == 1
    assert result.loc[result["threshold"] == 50, "sample_count"].iloc[0] == 6


def test_rapor_uygun_alternatifi_onerir_ama_otomatik_degistirmez() -> None:
    config = ThresholdCalibrationConfig(
        current_threshold=45,
        alternative_thresholds=(50, 55),
        minimum_sample_size=3,
        minimum_coverage_pct=50.0,
        minimum_quality_gain=3.0,
    )

    report = generate_calibration_report(records_table(), config)

    assert report.recommendation.recommended_threshold in (50, 55)
    assert report.recommendation.automatic_change_applied is False
    assert "otomatik" in report.recommendation.reason.lower()


def test_yetersiz_orneklemde_mevcut_esik_korunur() -> None:
    config = ThresholdCalibrationConfig(
        current_threshold=45,
        alternative_thresholds=(50,),
        minimum_sample_size=100,
    )

    report = generate_calibration_report(records_table(), config)

    assert report.recommendation.recommended_threshold == 45
    assert report.recommendation.decision == "Yetersiz örneklem"


def test_bos_veri_guvenli_rapor_uretir() -> None:
    report = generate_calibration_report(
        pd.DataFrame(),
        ThresholdCalibrationConfig(),
    )

    assert report.comparison.empty
    assert report.recommendation.decision == "Yetersiz veri"


def test_eksik_sutun_reddedilir() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="performance_label"):
        compare_thresholds(
            pd.DataFrame({"score": [45]}),
            ThresholdCalibrationConfig(),
        )


def test_gecersiz_esik_reddedilir() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ThresholdCalibrationConfig(current_threshold=101)

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_v2_calibration_gate.py
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_v2_decision_history import (
    PeriodComparison,
    PeriodComparisonReport,
    PeriodSummary,
)
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_v2_calibration_gate import (
    CalibrationGatePolicy,
    DeteriorationSignal,
    kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir,
    kalibrasyon_kapisi_raporunu_metne_cevir,
)


def _summary(pid):
    return PeriodSummary(pid, 3, "Modeli koru", 0.67, 0.0, 50.0, 30.0, 2.0)


def _report(result):
    return PeriodComparisonReport(
        current_period="P2",
        previous_period="P1",
        comparison=result,
        reasons=(),
        current_summary=_summary("P2"),
        previous_summary=_summary("P1"),
        summary="",
    )


def test_hic_gecmis_yoksa_kapi_kapali():
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([])
    assert r.calibration_review_open is False
    assert r.signal is DeteriorationSignal.YOK


def test_iki_karsilastirma_yetersiz():
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
    ])
    assert r.calibration_review_open is False
    assert r.signal is DeteriorationSignal.IZLE


def test_uc_karsilastirmada_iki_kotulesme_ve_son_kotuyse_kapi_acilir():
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.BENZER),
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
    ])
    assert r.calibration_review_open is True
    assert r.signal is DeteriorationSignal.KALICI_BOZULMA


def test_son_donem_kotu_degilse_kapi_acilmaz():
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.BENZER),
    ])
    assert r.calibration_review_open is False


def test_hic_kotulesme_yoksa_sinyal_yok():
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([
        _report(PeriodComparison.BENZER),
        _report(PeriodComparison.IYILESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.BENZER),
    ])
    assert r.signal is DeteriorationSignal.YOK
    assert r.calibration_review_open is False


def test_ozel_politika_desteklenir():
    policy = CalibrationGatePolicy(
        minimum_comparisons=2,
        minimum_worsening_count=1,
        require_latest_worsening=False,
    )
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.BENZER),
    ], policy=policy)
    assert r.calibration_review_open is True


def test_metin_kapi_durumunu_icerir():
    r = kalibrasyon_inceleme_kapisini_degerlendir([
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
        _report(PeriodComparison.BENZER),
        _report(PeriodComparison.KOTULESIYOR),
    ])
    text = kalibrasyon_kapisi_raporunu_metne_cevir(r)
    assert "V2 GERÇEK KULLANIM - KALİBRASYON İNCELEME KAPISI" in text
    assert "Kalibrasyon incelemesi açık: EVET" in text

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_v2_calibration_validation.py
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_v2_calibration_gate import (
    CalibrationGateReport,
    DeteriorationSignal,
)
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_v2_calibration_validation import (
    CalibrationBeforeAfter,
    CalibrationValidationDecision,
    kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula,
    kalibrasyon_dogrulama_raporunu_metne_cevir,
)


def _gate(open_: bool):
    return CalibrationGateReport(
        comparison_count=3,
        worsening_count=2,
        latest_comparison=None,
        signal=DeteriorationSignal.KALICI_BOZULMA if open_ else DeteriorationSignal.YOK,
        calibration_review_open=open_,
        reasons=(),
        summary="",
    )


def _cmp(**kw):
    data = dict(
        success_rate_before=45,
        success_rate_after=50,
        false_positive_before=45,
        false_positive_after=38,
        portfolio_return_before=1,
        portfolio_return_after=3,
        max_drawdown_before=8,
        max_drawdown_after=8.5,
        candidate_count_before=100,
        candidate_count_after=85,
    )
    data.update(kw)
    return CalibrationBeforeAfter(**data)


def test_kapi_kapaliysa_degerlendirme_yapilmaz():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(False),
        comparison=_cmp(),
    )
    assert report.decision is CalibrationValidationDecision.KAPI_KAPALI


def test_guclu_iyilesme_uygulama_adayi():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(True),
        comparison=_cmp(),
    )
    assert report.decision is CalibrationValidationDecision.UYGULAMA_ADAYI


def test_iki_temel_bozulma_reddedilir():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(True),
        comparison=_cmp(
            success_rate_after=42,
            portfolio_return_after=-2,
        ),
    )
    assert report.decision is CalibrationValidationDecision.REDDET


def test_asiri_aday_kaybi_guvenlik_sinyali():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(True),
        comparison=_cmp(candidate_count_after=50),
    )
    assert report.candidate_loss_pct == 50.0
    assert report.decision is CalibrationValidationDecision.INCELE


def test_drawdown_bozulmasi_tek_basina_inceleme():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(True),
        comparison=_cmp(max_drawdown_after=11),
    )
    assert report.decision is CalibrationValidationDecision.INCELE


def test_sinirli_iyilesme_inceleme():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(True),
        comparison=_cmp(
            success_rate_after=46,
            false_positive_after=44,
            portfolio_return_after=1.5,
        ),
    )
    assert report.decision is CalibrationValidationDecision.INCELE


def test_metin_karari_icerir():
    report = kontrollu_kalibrasyonu_dogrula(
        gate_report=_gate(True),
        comparison=_cmp(),
    )
    text = kalibrasyon_dogrulama_raporunu_metne_cevir(report)
    assert "V2 GERÇEK KULLANIM - KONTROLLÜ KALİBRASYON DOĞRULAMASI" in text
    assert "Karar: Uygulama adayı" in text

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_validation.py
from datetime import date

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_validation import (
    PreBreakoutValidationConfig,
    dogrulama_ozeti,
    toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama,
    uygun_kesim_tarihleri,
)


def veri(offset: float = 0.0, gun: int = 140) -> pd.DataFrame:
    index = pd.bdate_range("2026-01-02", periods=gun)
    close = np.linspace(100.0 + offset, 118.0 + offset, gun)
    close += np.sin(np.linspace(0.0, 10.0, gun))
    spread = np.linspace(2.0, 1.0, gun)
    volume = np.full(gun, 2_000_000.0)
    volume[-30:] = 2_300_000.0

    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "Open": close - 0.2,
            "High": close + spread / 2,
            "Low": close - spread / 2,
            "Close": close,
            "Volume": volume,
        },
        index=index,
    )


def config() -> PreBreakoutValidationConfig:
    return PreBreakoutValidationConfig(
        start_date=date(2026, 4, 1),
        end_date=date(2026, 6, 30),
        cutoff_step=5,
        minimum_score=0,
    )


def test_config_gecersiz_tarih_araligini_reddeder() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PreBreakoutValidationConfig(
            start_date=date(2026, 7, 2),
            end_date=date(2026, 7, 1),
        )


def test_kesim_tarihleri_minimum_gecmis_ve_adimi_korur() -> None:
    dates = uygun_kesim_tarihleri(veri(), config())

    assert dates
    assert all(config().start_date <= item <= config().end_date for item in dates)


def test_toplu_dogrulama_deterministik_siralidir() -> None:
    data = {
        "B.IS": veri(3.0),
        "A.IS": veri(0.0),
    }

    first = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(data, config())
    second = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(data, config())

    assert first == second
    assert list(first) == sorted(
        first,
        key=lambda record: (record.cutoff_date, record.symbol),
    )


def test_minimum_skor_kayit_sayisini_filtreler() -> None:
    data = {"TEST.IS": veri()}
    low = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(data, config())

    high_config = PreBreakoutValidationConfig(
        start_date=config().start_date,
        end_date=config().end_date,
        cutoff_step=5,
        minimum_score=100,
    )
    high = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(data, high_config)

    assert len(low) >= len(high)
    assert high == ()


def test_dogrulama_ozeti_sayimlari_tutarlidir() -> None:
    records = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(
        {"TEST.IS": veri()},
        config(),
    )
    summary = dogrulama_ozeti(records)

    assert summary.total_replays == len(records)
    assert 0.0 <= summary.success_rate_pct <= 100.0
    assert 0.0 <= summary.false_positive_rate_pct <= 100.0

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_validation_evidence.py
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
import json
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_validation_evidence import build_prebreakout_validation_evidence_rows, persist_prebreakout_validation_evidence
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Replay: momentum_score: float; trend_score: float; volume_ratio: float; resistance_distance_pct: float; risk_reward: float
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Record: symbol: str; cutoff_date: date; performance_label: str; replay: Replay
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Result: records: tuple[Record,...]
def test_build_rows_reads_existing_result_records():
    r=Result(records=(Record("TEST",date(2026,8,17),"SUCCESS",Replay(70,12,1.8,1.2,2.0)),))
    rows=build_prebreakout_validation_evidence_rows(r)
    assert len(rows)==1 and rows[0]["symbol"]=="TEST" and rows[0]["performance_label"]=="SUCCESS" and rows[0]["replay.risk_reward"]==2.0
def test_persist_writes_json_and_csv_sidecars(tmp_path):
    r=Result(records=(Record("TEST",date(2026,8,17),"FALSE_POSITIVE",Replay(60,9,1.1,2.5,1.3)),))
    written=persist_prebreakout_validation_evidence(r,output_dir=tmp_path)
    assert written is not None
    jp,cp=written; assert jp.exists() and cp.exists(); data=json.loads(jp.read_text(encoding="utf-8")); assert data[0]["symbol"]=="TEST"

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_prebreakout_validation_no_lookahead.py
from datetime import date

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_validation import (
    PreBreakoutValidationConfig,
    toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama,
)


def veri() -> pd.DataFrame:
    index = pd.bdate_range("2026-01-02", periods=140)
    close = np.linspace(100.0, 114.0, 140)
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "Open": close - 0.2,
            "High": close + 0.8,
            "Low": close - 0.8,
            "Close": close,
            "Volume": np.full(140, 2_000_000.0),
        },
        index=index,
    )


def test_gelecek_verisi_degisse_bile_kesim_gunu_analizi_degismez() -> None:
    original = veri()
    changed = original.copy()

    cutoff = pd.Timestamp("2026-05-15")
    future_mask = changed.index > cutoff
    changed.loc[future_mask, ["Open", "High", "Low", "Close"]] *= 2.0

    config = PreBreakoutValidationConfig(
        start_date=cutoff.date(),
        end_date=cutoff.date(),
        cutoff_step=1,
        minimum_score=0,
    )

    first = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(
        {"TEST.IS": original},
        config,
    )
    second = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(
        {"TEST.IS": changed},
        config,
    )

    assert len(first) == len(second) == 1
    assert first[0].replay.analysis == second[0].replay.analysis
    assert first[0].replay.entry_close == second[0].replay.entry_close

from datetime import date

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_validation import (
    PreBreakoutValidationConfig,
    dogrulama_ozeti,
    toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama,
)
from backend.app.prebreakout.prebreakout_validation_report import (
    asama_bazli_ozet,
    dogrulama_kayitlarini_tabloya_cevir,
    dogrulama_ozetini_tabloya_cevir,
)


def veri() -> pd.DataFrame:
    index = pd.bdate_range("2026-01-02", periods=140)
    close = np.linspace(100.0, 118.0, 140)
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "Open": close - 0.2,
            "High": close + 0.8,
            "Low": close - 0.8,
            "Close": close,
            "Volume": np.full(140, 2_000_000.0),
        },
        index=index,
    )


def test_dogrulama_raporu_ileri_performans_sutunlari_icerir() -> None:
    config = PreBreakoutValidationConfig(
        start_date=date(2026, 4, 1),
        end_date=date(2026, 5, 30),
        cutoff_step=10,
        minimum_score=0,
    )
    records = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(
        {"TEST.IS": veri()},

    )

    table = dogrulama_kayitlarini_tabloya_cevir(records)

    assert "performance_label" in table.columns
    assert "max_return_20d_pct" in table.columns


def test_ozet_ve_asama_tablolari_uretilir() -> None:
    config = PreBreakoutValidationConfig(
        start_date=date(2026, 4, 1),
        end_date=date(2026, 5, 30),
        cutoff_step=10,
        minimum_score=0,
    )
    records = toplu_tarihsel_dogrulama(
        {"TEST.IS": veri()},
        config,
    )
    summary = dogrulama_ozeti(records)

    summary_table = dogrulama_ozetini_tabloya_cevir(summary)
    stage_table = asama_bazli_ozet(records)

    assert len(summary_table) == 1
    assert "success_rate_pct" in summary_table.columns
    assert "stage" in stage_table.columns

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_ranking_validation.py
from backend.app.analysis.ranking_memory import RankObservation
from backend.app.analysis.ranking_validation import (
    build_validation_outcomes,
    evaluate_entry_outcomes,
    summarize_outcomes,
)


def obs(day, rank, symbol="AAA", size=500, source="BIST", profile="default"):
    return RankObservation(
        as_of=day,
        symbol=symbol,
        rank=rank,
        ranked_universe_size=size,
        universe_source=source,
        profile_name=profile,
    )


def history():
    ranks = [400, 300, 220, 150, 110, 80, 70, 60, 55, 48, 42]
    days = [f"2026-08-{day:02d}" for day in range(1, 12)]
    return tuple(obs(day, rank) for day, rank in zip(days, ranks))


def test_outcomes_cover_1_3_5_10_sessions():
    outcomes = evaluate_entry_outcomes(
        history(),
        symbol="AAA",
        entry_as_of="2026-08-01",
    )
    assert [item.horizon_sessions for item in outcomes] == [1, 3, 5, 10]
    assert [item.outcome_rank for item in outcomes] == [300, 150, 80, 42]


def test_top_zone_conversion_is_measured_not_used_as_signal():
    outcomes = evaluate_entry_outcomes(
        history(),
        symbol="AAA",
        entry_as_of="2026-08-01",
    )
    by_horizon = {item.horizon_sessions: item for item in outcomes}
    assert by_horizon[5].reached_top100 is True
    assert by_horizon[10].reached_top50 is True
    assert not hasattr(by_horizon[10], "decision")
    assert not hasattr(by_horizon[10], "signal")


def test_normalized_gain_handles_universe_size_change():
    items = (
        obs("2026-08-01", 400, size=600),
        obs("2026-08-02", 200, size=500),
    )
    result = evaluate_entry_outcomes(
        items,
        symbol="AAA",
        entry_as_of="2026-08-01",
    )[0]
    assert result.available is True
    assert result.rank_gain == 200
    assert result.normalized_gain is not None
    assert result.normalized_gain > 0


def test_source_change_marks_outcome_unavailable():
    items = (
        obs("2026-08-01", 400, source="OLD"),
        obs("2026-08-02", 100, source="BIST"),
    )
    result = evaluate_entry_outcomes(
        items,
        symbol="AAA",
        entry_as_of="2026-08-01",
    )[0]
    assert result.available is False
    assert result.rank_gain is None


def test_summary_reports_conversion_retention_and_unavailable_rate():
    all_outcomes = build_validation_outcomes(
        history(),
        entries=(("AAA", "2026-08-01"),),
    )
    summary = summarize_outcomes(all_outcomes, horizon_sessions=5)
    assert summary.total_entries == 1
    assert summary.evaluated_outcomes == 1
    assert summary.top100_conversion_rate == 1.0
    assert summary.positive_rank_gain_rate == 1.0
    assert summary.retention_rate == 1.0
    assert summary.unavailable_rate == 0.0


def test_future_horizon_stays_pending_without_fake_result():
    outcomes = evaluate_entry_outcomes(
        history()[:4],
        symbol="AAA",
        entry_as_of="2026-08-01",
    )
    ten = next(item for item in outcomes if item.horizon_sessions == 10)
    assert ten.available is False
    assert ten.outcome_rank is None
    assert ten.rank_gain is None

FILE=D:\Projects\Hisse-Robotu\backend\tests\test_v13_calibration_closeout.py
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from backend.app.analysis.shadow_portfolio import ShadowPosition
from backend.app.analysis.signal_outcome_tracking import (
    HorizonOutcome,
    SignalOutcome,
    SignalSnapshot,
)
from backend.app.analysis.success_comparison_report import (
    build_success_comparison_report,
)
from backend.app.analysis.v13_calibration_closeout import (
    DEVAM,
    KALIBRASYON_GEREKLI,
    TEMKINLI_DEVAM,
    build_v13_calibration_decision,
    criterion_rows,
    decision_summary_rows,
)
from backend.app.data.delayed_data_pilot import PilotLogRecord


def pilot(index: int, *, locked: bool = False) -> PilotLogRecord:
    return PilotLogRecord(
        symbol="THYAO.IS",
        source="yfinance",
        exchange_time=f"2026-07-31T14:{index:02d}:00+03:00",
        received_time=f"2026-07-31T14:{index + 15:02d}:00+03:00",
        measured_delay_minutes=15.0,
        expected_delay_minutes=15,
        status="Gün içi ön izleme",
        locked=locked,
        preview_only=True,
        last_price=100.0,
        cumulative_volume=100000.0,
        latest_bar_volume=10000.0,
        latest_bar_completed=False,
        missing_bar_count=0,
        price_change_percent=1.0,
        error_message="",
    )


def outcome(symbol: str, return_percent: float) -> SignalOutcome:
    snapshot = SignalSnapshot(
        symbol=symbol,
        signal_date=date(2026, 7, 1),
        reference_price=100.0,
        decision_class="İzlemeye Değer",
        quality_score=80,
        daily_direction="Pozitif",
        weekly_direction="Pozitif",
        market_regime="Pozitif",
        sector="Banka",
        momentum_state="Pozitif MACD",
        volume_confirmation=True,
    )
    horizons = tuple(
        HorizonOutcome(
            horizon=horizon,
            available=True,
            end_date=date(2026, 7, horizon),
            close_return_percent=return_percent,
            maximum_favorable_excursion_percent=return_percent + 2,
            maximum_adverse_excursion_percent=-1.0,
            target_1_hit=True,
            target_2_hit=return_percent > 5,
            stop_hit=False,
        )
        for horizon in (5, 10, 20)
    )
    return SignalOutcome(
        snapshot=snapshot,
        horizons=horizons,
        evaluated_at="2026-07-31T12:00:00+00:00",
    )


def closed_position(index: int) -> ShadowPosition:
    return ShadowPosition(
        symbol=f"SYM{index}",
        entry_date=date(2026, 7, 1),
        entry_price=100.0,
        quantity=10,
        capital_at_entry=10000.0,
        status="Kapalı",
        exit_date=date(2026, 7, 15),
        exit_price=105.0,
        close_reason="Test",
    )


def test_tum_esikler_gecilince_devam_karari_uretilir():
    outcomes = tuple(outcome(f"SYM{i}", 4.0) for i in range(5))
    report = build_success_comparison_report(
        outcomes,
        minimum_sample_size=5,
    )

    decision = build_v13_calibration_decision(
        pilot_records=tuple(pilot(i) for i in range(5)),
        signal_outcomes=outcomes,
        shadow_positions=tuple(closed_position(i) for i in range(5)),
        comparison_report=report,
        minimum_sufficient_comparisons=3,
    )

    assert decision.decision == DEVAM
    assert decision.failed_critical_count == 0
    assert decision.warning_count == 0


def test_kritik_veri_eksiginde_kalibrasyon_gerekli_karari_uretilir():
    report = build_success_comparison_report(
        (),
        minimum_sample_size=5,
    )

    decision = build_v13_calibration_decision(
        pilot_records=(),
        signal_outcomes=(),
        shadow_positions=(),
        comparison_report=report,
    )

    assert decision.decision == KALIBRASYON_GEREKLI
    assert decision.failed_critical_count > 0


def test_kritik_zincirler_gecerli_ama_orneklem_azsa_temkinli_devam():
    outcomes = tuple(outcome(f"SYM{i}", 4.0) for i in range(5))
    report = build_success_comparison_report(
        outcomes,
        minimum_sample_size=5,
    )

    decision = build_v13_calibration_decision(
        pilot_records=tuple(pilot(i) for i in range(5)),
        signal_outcomes=outcomes,
        shadow_positions=(),
        comparison_report=report,
        minimum_sufficient_comparisons=999,
    )

    assert decision.decision == TEMKINLI_DEVAM
    assert decision.failed_critical_count == 0
    assert decision.warning_count > 0


def test_kapanis_tablolari_uretilir():
    outcomes = tuple(outcome(f"SYM{i}", 4.0) for i in range(5))
    report = build_success_comparison_report(
        outcomes,
        minimum_sample_size=5,
    )
    decision = build_v13_calibration_decision(
        pilot_records=tuple(pilot(i) for i in range(5)),
        signal_outcomes=outcomes,
        shadow_positions=tuple(closed_position(i) for i in range(5)),
        comparison_report=report,
    )

    assert criterion_rows(decision)
    assert decision_summary_rows(decision)
    assert decision_summary_rows(decision)[0]["Metrik"] == "Kalibrasyon kararı"
